Date:22-02-2024.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No. RD 572 KAMGRARA 2017
Bengalur Dated:22-09-2017 regarding altering the limits of Akkirampura village of
Koratagere Taluk and Tumkur District and constituting a new revenue village called
Kadilapura and Hosahalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
No. RD 572 KAMGRARA 2017 Bengalur Dated:09-11-2017 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Tumkur district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 12-03-2018 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of
Karnataka here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Akkiramapura of Koratagere Taluk and Tumkur District to form a new
(7254)

gen Ya BMFUT WBA, Awesewd, OO, Ayowr, [09% 253333
revenue village called as Kadilapura of Koratagere Taluk in Tumkur District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

E- 1282484 Multi Storied Building

Date: 21-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of K YADATHORE village of
Heggadadevanakote Taluk, Mysuru District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called UDBOORU COLONY as specified in schedule along with maps
with old survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Mysuru District.

district along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 40 PFH 2023
Dated: 10.07.2023 regarding altering the limits of Udugere village of
KadurTaluk in Chikkamagaluru District and constituting a new revenue village
called Sannenahalli & Nayakanagara was published as required by section 6 of the
Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka
Gazette No. RD 40PFH 2023 Dated:14.08.2023 inviting objections or suggestions
from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its
publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner
Chikkamagaluru district.

And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 14.08.2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;

Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE – 1

Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Udugere of KadurTaluk in Chikkamagalur District to form a new revenue

village called as Sannenahalli of Kadurtaluk and Chikkamagalur District along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 56GLH 2022 dated04-07-2022
regarding altering the limits of Changadarahalli village of Hanuru Taluk and
Chamarajanagar District and constituting a new revenue village called
Poojaribhovidoddi was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
No RD 56GLH 2022 dated:04-07-2022 inviting objections or suggestions from all the
persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Chamarajanagar
district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 15-07-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village CHANGADARAHALLI of HANURU taluk in CHAMARAJANAGARA
District to form a new revenue village called as POOJARIBHOVIDODDI of
HANURU taluk CHAMARAJANAGARA District along with the survey numbers
included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 22 PFH 2023, Dated : 21-02-2023
regarding altering the limits of Mummigatti village of Dharwad Taluk and Dharwad
District and constituting a new revenue village called Renukanagar was published as
required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12
of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No157 Part IV-A dated : 21-02-2023 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within

thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Dharwad district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 25-07-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Mummigatti of Dharwad Taluk and Dharwad District to form a new revenue
village called as Renukanagar of Dharwad Taluk and Dharwad District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/8/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1281042 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 16-02-2024
PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka

propose s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Basuru village of Kadur
Taluk of Chikmagaluru district to convert the existing Boragondanhalli as specified
in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of of
Basuru village for conversion of Boragondanhalli unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be
affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after
thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chikmagaluru District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called Boragondanhalli village Kadur
talluk of Chikmagaluru District convert the same to form hamlet/extension within the
boundary of Basuru Revenue village and same will here in after be named as called
Boragondanhalli of Basuru village Kadur taluk, Chikmagaluru District.

It is hereby declared that the extent of land indicated in the columns(4) above
with survey numbers specified in column (3) on which the said unrecorded habitation
is located is proposed to be converted as habitation area (Gramatana) of
Boragondanahalli hamlet/part of Basuru village in Kadur taluk of Chikamagaluru
district
The proposal specified above shall come into effect from such date as
the State Government may specify by Notification issued under section 5 of the

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No. No.RD 558 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated: 08-12-2017 regarding altering the limits of Negalala village of Koratagere
Taluk in Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village called as
Maripalya sub village was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette
No.RD 558 KAMGRARA 2017 dated 18-01-2018 inviting objections or
suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from
the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 01-04-2018.

And whereas, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of Maripalya village of Koratagere Taluk
of Tumkuru District convert the same to form hamlet with in the boundary of
Negalala revenue village and same will here in after be named as called Maripalya of
Negalala village Koratagere Taluk of Tumkuru District

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 32 PFH 2023
Dated 13-03-2023 regarding altering the limits of Avaradi village of Ramdurg Taluk
and Belagavi district and constituting a new revenue village called Shivapet was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 32 PFH 2023 dated
16-03-2023 inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected
thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette
addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 16-03-2023 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the
areas specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of
Avaradi Village of RAMDURG Taluk BELAGAVI District to from a new

revenue village called as Shivapet of RAMDURG Taluk and BELAGAVI District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) threrof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Yalahanka Village of
Halebeedu Hobli,Belur Taluk, Hassan District and to constitute a new revenue
villages called Byadarahalli as specified in schedule along with maps with old
survey numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Hassan District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Yalahanka of Halebeedu Hobli, Belur Taluk, in Hassan District to form a
new revenue village called Byadarahalli of Halebeedu Hobli, Belur Taluk, in Hassan
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose
s to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Ainapur Village of Vijayapur taluk
,Vijayapur District and to constitute a new revenue villages called L R Nayak
Nagar as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and
new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Vijayapur
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Ainapur Village of Vijayapur taluk Vijayapur District to form a new
revenue village called L R Nayak Nagar of Vijayapur taluk Vijayapur District
along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the, Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Uppinahalli Village of
Kadur taluk Chikmagaluru District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called TheerthaNagara as specified in schedule.
Notification is hereby issued that Land bearing survey numbers and extent
specified in Schedule shall be declared as habitation area within the boundary of
Uppinahalli village for conversion of TheerthaNagara unrecorded habitation in to
hamlet/ extension of the said revenue village.

Now , therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said
Act, notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to
be affected thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or
after thirty days from the date of publication of the notification in the official
Gazette.

Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the
date Specified above will be considered by the state Government.
Objection or suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy
Commissioner, Chikkamagaluru District.

SCHEDULE
Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column (3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area of called TheerthaNagara village of
Kadur Taluk of Chikmagaluru District convert the same to form hamlet/part within
the boundary of Uppinahalli Revenue village and same will here in after be named as
called TheerthaNagara of Uppinahalli village in Kadur Taluk of Chikmagaluru
District.

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 127 PFH 2022, Bengaluru, Dated
11-07-2022 regarding altering the limits of K.K.Koppa village of Belagavi Taluk and
Belagavi District and constituting a new revenue village called Nagerahal was
published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964
(Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. 154 dated 02-08-2022
inviting objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby
within thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to
the Deputy Commissioner Belagavi district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 02-08-2022 And whereas, objections and suggestions have been received and
considered by the State Government;

Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA
RC-CFS0ADMN/160/2023-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E- 1270955 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date: 17-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Dhanagere Village of
Kollegala Taluk, Chamarajnagar District and to constitute a new revenue villages
called Cheluvanahalli as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected

thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Chamarajnagar
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
DhanagereVillage of KollegalaTaluk,Chamarajnagardistrict to form a New revenue
village called as Cheluvanahalliof KollegalaTaluk and ChamarajnagarDistrict along
with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

FINAL NOTIFICATION

Whereas the draft of the Notification No RD 05(06) KAMGRARA 2021 Dated
06-12-2022 regarding altering the limits of Veeradevanahalli village of Belur Taluk
and Hassan District and constituting a new revenue village called Ammana
Byadarahalli was published as required by section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 05(06)
KAMGRARA 2021 dated 05-01-2023 inviting objections or suggestions from all the

persons likely to be affected thereby within thirty days from the date of its publication
in the Official Gazette addressed to the Deputy Commissioner Hassan district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 12-12-2022 And whereas, no objections and suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village Veeradevanahalli village of Belur Taluk and Hassan District to form a new
revenue village called as Ammana Byadarahalli village of Belur Taluk and Hassan
District along with the survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.

E-1316729 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:23-02-2024

FINAL NOTIFICATION
Whereas the draft of the Notification No.RD 567 KAMGRARA 2017
Dated: 08-12-2017 regarding altering the limits of G Nagenahalli village of
Koratagere Taluk and Tumkuru District and constituting a new revenue sub village
called as G.Nagenahalli Gollarahatti sub village was published as required by
section 6 of the Karnataka Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) in
the Karnataka Gazette No. RD 567 KAMGRARA 2017 dated: 18-01-2018 inviting
objections or suggestions from all the persons likely to be affected thereby within
thirty days from the date of its publication in the Official Gazette addressed to the
Deputy Commissioner Tumkuru district.
And whereas, the said gazette was made available to the public on
Dated: 27-03-2018 And where as, no objections or suggestions have been received;
Now, therefore, in exercise of the powers conferred by section 5 of Karnataka
Land Revenue Act, 1964 (Karnataka act No.12 of 1964) the Government of Karnataka
here by makes the following notification, namely:-
SCHEDULE

Land bearing survey numbers and extent specified below in column(3) of the
table shall be declared as habitation area ofcalled G Nagenahalli Gollarahatti
village of Koratagere taluk of Tumkuru District convert the same to form
hamlet/part within the boundary of G Nagenahalli revenue village and same will
here in after be named as G.Nagenahalli Gollarahatti of G Nagenahalli village in
Koratagere Taluka of Tumkuru District.

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration
the factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land
Revenue Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand
under land revenue in the village and other head of the account and having
regard to the administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka
proposes to issue a Notification to alter the limits of Kuppe village of Saligrama
taluk, Mysuru District and to constitute a new revenue villages called
Muddanahalli as specified in schedule along with maps with old survey
numbers and new assigned to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date

Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Mysuru District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Saligrama Taluk in Mysuru district to form a new village called as
Muddanahalli of Krishnarajanagara Taluk and Mysuru district along with the survey
numbers included in column (4) thereof

GOVERNMENT OF KARNATAKA

RC- CFS0ADMN/73/2024-RC Karnataka Government Secretariat
E-1261396 Multi Storied Building
Bengaluru 560-001,
Date:20-02-2024

PRELIMINARY NOTIFICATION

In exercise of the powers conferred by Section 5 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Act 1964 ( Karnataka act no 12 of 1964) and taking into consideration the
factors Specified in clauses ( i ) to ( iv ) of rule 3 of the Karnataka Land Revenue
Rules 1966 such as the population, area of the village the demand under land
revenue in the village and other head of the account and having regard to the
administrative convenience the Government of Karnataka propose s to issue a
Notification to alter the limits of Gummaghatta Village of Pavagada Taluk,
Tumkur District and to constitute a new revenue Channamareddyhalli as
specified in schedule along with maps with old survey numbers and new assigned
to them.

Now, therefore in exercise of the power conferred by section 6 of the said Act,
notice is hereby given for the information of all the persons likely to be affected
thereby that the proposal will be taken into consideration on or after thirty days
from the date of publication of the notification in the official Gazette.
Any objection or suggestion which may be received by the state
Government from any person with respect to the said proposal before the date
Specified above will be considered by the state Government. Objection or
suggestion may be addressed to the Deputy Commissioner, Tumkur
District.

SCHEDULE – 1
Lands bearing survey numbers of the villages and survey numbers of the areas
specified in column (2) of the table below shall be excluded from the limits of the said
village of Tumkur District Pavagada Taluk to form a new revenue village called
Channamareddyhalli Village of Pavagada Taluk, Tumkur District along with the
survey numbers included in column (4) thereof.
